Qt6.11.3 official source notice index

The 12 complete original module tar.xz archives are retained and every archive SHA256 matches its retained official .meta4 digest. Full source acquisition and independent Qt rebuild are separate facts: source archives are complete; independent rebuild is not verified.
No complete binary-embedded credits page was extracted. The notices come from original source text and ten explicitly labeled exact-revision upstream supplements. Existing failed binary-resource attempts were not retried or reclassified as success.

Coverage: all named source LICENSE/COPYING/NOTICE/COPYRIGHT/OFL/MIT/BSD/AUTHORS/CONTRIBUTORS/README.chromium/qt_attribution and LICENSES/ texts are included. All local Qt attribution LicenseFile paths resolve. Explicit Shipped: no declarations, metadata templates, negative test fixtures, Not Included sentinels, absent platform SDK payloads, conditional standalone dependencies and canonical shared-component license paths are documented individually. OpenXR metadata and DEPS pin differences are preserved, with supplements at both revisions.
